Model before rewriting:

find exception: set (maxSize 2) of int(-2, 3)
find x: set (maxSize 2) of int(-2, 3)
find y: set (maxSize 2) of int(-2, 3)
find allowed: bool
find fixed: bool
find negative: bool
find ordinary: bool
find repeated: bool

such that

allDifferentExcept([x,y,{-2};int(1..3)], exception),
(ordinary) <-> (allDifferent([x,y;int(1..2)])),
(allowed) <-> (allDifferentExcept([x,y;int(1..2)], exception)),
(repeated) <-> (allDifferentExcept([x,x,y;int(1..3)], exception)),
(negative) <-> (!(allDifferent([x,y;int(1..2)]))),
(fixed) <-> (allDifferentExcept([x,y;int(1..2)], {-2})),
or([allDifferentExcept([x,y;int(1..2)], exception),!(allDifferent([x,y;int(1..2)]));int(1..)])
